Code Sample: pythonCopyEditimport jaydebeapi

# Path to the JDBC jar file
jdbc_driver_path = "/path/to/presto-jdbc.jar"  # Replace with your JDBC jar file path

# ...
Related Explanation: Preceding explanation: Here’s an example Python code that connects to Presto/Trino using JayDeBeApi:
Following explanation: Key Points:
List instructions:
JDBC URL: The URL for Presto would look like jdbc:presto://<host>:<port>/<catalog>/<schema>. In this case, Hive is used as the catalog.


Driver Class: Use the correct driver class based on whether you're connecting to Presto or Trino.

Following explanation: Notes:
List instructions:
JAR Path: Replace /path/to/trino-jdbc.jar with the actual path where the Trino JDBC driver is located on your machine.


JDBC URL: The URL must match the format jdbc:trino://<host>:<port>/<catalog>/<schema>. Adjust the IP, port, and schema as needed.


Authentication: If your Trino server doesn't require a password, you can omit the "password" field in the props dictionary.


SSL: If your connection requires SSL, ensure that "SSL": "true" is included in the properties. Otherwise, you can remove that option.
